## 95. RtsqA5::RefreshInfo

推进 SQE/Task计数：EAGER可立即提交，BATCH 达内部阈值仍会提交

完整范围：[rtsq_a5.cc:L464–L495](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L464-L495)；文件：`hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc`。

功能与分支：

- [S339 / L473](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L473)：编译条件`ifdef CCL_KERNEL_AICPU`限定后续实现，区分Host/设备或构建能力分支。

- [S340 / L475](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L475)：仅当`(launchFlag_ && !IsBatchLaunchMode())`成立时进入此分支；调用IsBatchLaunchMode。

- [S344 / L483](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L483)：结束前述编译条件控制的实现片段。

- [S346 / L486](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/stream/aicpu/rtsq_a5.cc#L486)：仅当`(pendingSqeCnt != PER_LAUNCH_SQE_CNT)`（本地待提交SQE条数）成立时进入此分支。

## 114. UbTransportLiteImpl::GetRmtRmaBufSliceLite

在远端已交换内存范围中解析地址/长度/token；无匹配抛异常

完整范围：[ub_transport_lite_impl.cc:L296–L321](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc#L296-L321)；文件：`hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc`。

功能与分支：

- [S290 / L303](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc#L303)：在`(it != rmtBufferMap.begin())`（远端交换得到的注册范围映射的begin字段）条件下重复执行后续等待或分片处理。

- [S293 / L309](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc#L309)：仅当`(iterBuf.Contains(rmtBuf.GetAddr(), rmtBuf.GetSize()))`成立时进入此分支；判断请求地址和完整字节范围是否包含在此已注册区内；读取缓冲区起始地址；读取缓冲区字节长度。

## 117. UbTransportLiteImpl::Post

完整范围：[ub_transport_lite_impl.cc:L428–L518](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc#L428-L518)；文件：`hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc`。

功能与分支：

- [S374 / L434](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc#L434)：仅当`(index == 1)`（条目下标）成立时进入此分支。

- [S392 / L464](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc#L464)：设置本地待提交SQE条数为/按`needCacheTask ? stream.GetRtsq()->GetPendingSqeCnt() : 0`（是否记录本轮任务缓存、承载任务的执行流的GetRtsq字段）；返回当前StreamLite持有的具体执行队列；读取Doorbell生成之前当前执行队列的待提交SQE数。

- [S405 / L482](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc#L482)：仅当`(needCacheTask && isReportTask)`（是否记录本轮任务缓存、是否生成任务观测记录）成立时进入此分支。

## 120. UbTransportLiteImpl::ReadReduce

构造读取归约 WQE，再组织 Doorbell SQE 与归约观测

完整范围：[ub_transport_lite_impl.cc:L845–L913](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc#L845-L913)；文件：`hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc`。

功能与分支：

- [S723 / L871](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc#L871)：设置本地待提交SQE条数为/按`needCacheTask ? stream.GetRtsq()->GetPendingSqeCnt() : 0`（是否记录本轮任务缓存、承载任务的执行流的GetRtsq字段）；返回当前StreamLite持有的具体执行队列；读取Doorbell生成之前当前执行队列的待提交SQE数。

- [S737 / L891](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc#L891)：仅当`(needCacheTask && isReportTask)`（是否记录本轮任务缓存、是否生成任务观测记录）成立时进入此分支。

## 121. UbTransportLiteImpl::BatchTransfer

传统批数据接口按 WRITE/WRITE_REDUCE/READ/READ_REDUCE 构造 WQE，仅末项 CQE

完整范围：[ub_transport_lite_impl.cc:L1019–L1137](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc#L1019-L1137)；文件：`hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc`。

功能与分支：

- [S858 / L1027](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc#L1027)：仅当`(UNLIKELY(loc.empty()))`（本端缓冲区/地址的empty字段）成立时进入此分支；检查容器是否没有登记项。

- [S874 / L1053](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc#L1053)：设置本地待提交SQE条数为/按`needCacheTask ? stream.GetRtsq()->GetPendingSqeCnt() : 0`（是否记录本轮任务缓存、承载任务的执行流的GetRtsq字段）；返回当前StreamLite持有的具体执行队列；读取Doorbell生成之前当前执行队列的待提交SQE数。

- [S877 / L1058](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc#L1058)：按`(u32 i = 0; i < insNum; i++)`（本批条目下标、批项数量）遍历本批条目/分片；各次处理保持数组对应关系。

- [S878 / L1060](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc#L1060)：设置UB WQE保序/完成配置的cqeEn字段为/按`(i == insNum - 1) ? true : false`（本批条目下标、批项数量）。

- [S885 / L1073](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc#L1073)：仅当`(transferOp[i].transType == TransferType::WRITE)`（批操作种类/归约描述数组、本批条目下标）成立时进入此分支。

- [S887 / L1077](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc#L1077)：仅当`(transferOp[i].transType == TransferType::WRITE_REDUCE)`（批操作种类/归约描述数组、本批条目下标）成立时进入此分支。

完整范围：[ub_transport_lite_impl.cc:L1234–L1334](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc#L1234-L1334)；文件：`hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc`。

## 124. UbTransportLiteImpl::ExecuteBatchTransfer

逐条解析传输/通知描述并组装 slice、操作和通知索引数组

完整范围：[ub_transport_lite_impl.cc:L1337–L1453](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc#L1337-L1453)；文件：`hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc`。

功能与分支：

- [S1070 / L1361](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc#L1361)：按`(uint32_t i = 0; i < transferDescNum; i++)`（本批条目下标、批操作描述条数）遍历本批条目/分片；各次处理保持数组对应关系。

- [S1080 / L1381](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc#L1381)：仅当`(tfType != Hccl::TransferType::NOTIFY_RECORD)`（底层传输种类）成立时进入此分支。

- [S1092 / L1405](https://github.com/zstar1003/hccl-hcomm-annotated/blob/824a8a80731bd66ef6eb78891b9aec0acfca281c/hcomm/src/legacy/ascend950/unified_platform/resource/transport/aicpu/ub_transport_lite_impl.cc#L1405)：仅当`(tfType == Hccl::TransferType::NOTIFY_RECORD || tfType == Hccl::TransferType::WRITE_WITH_NOTIFY`（底层传输种类）成立时进入此分支。
